[S2CID](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [30688575](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").*
9. [↑](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") Chomskyan linguistics encourages the investigation of "[corner cases](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")" that stress the limits of its theoretical models (comparable to [pathological](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") phenomena in mathematics), typically created using [thought experiments](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), rather than the systematic investigation of typical phenomena that occur in real-world data, as is the case in [corpus linguistics](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").  The creation and use of such [corpora](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") of real-world data is a fundamental part of machine-learning algorithms for natural language processing.  In addition, theoretical underpinnings of Chomskyan linguistics such as the so-called "[poverty of the stimulus](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=ext.cite.parsoid.styles%7Cext.cite.styles%7Cext.math.styles%7Cext.uls.interlanguage%7Cext.visualEditor.desktopArticleTarget.noscript%7Cext.wikimediaBadges%7Cext.wikimediaBadges.ulsV2%7Cext.wikimediamessages.styles%7Cjquery.makeCollapsible.styles%7Cmediawiki.skinning.content.parsoid%7Cskins.vector.icons%2Cstyles%7Cskins.vector.search.codex.styles%7Cwikibase.client.init&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")" argument entail that general learning algorithms, as are typically used in machine learning, cannot be successful in language processing.  As a result, the Chomskyan paradigm discouraged the application of such models to language processing.

- *Bates, M (1995). [Bibcode](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"):[1995PNAS...92.9977B](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"). [doi](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"):[10.1073/pnas.92.22.9977](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"). [PMC](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [40721](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"). [PMID](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [7479812](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").*
- Steven Bird, Ewan Klein, and Edward Loper (2009). *Natural Language Processing with Python*. O'Reilly Media. [ISBN](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [978-0-596-51649-9](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
- [Kenna Hughes-Castleberry](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"), "A Murder Mystery Puzzle: The literary puzzle *[Cain's Jawbone](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")*, which has stumped humans for decades, reveals the limitations of natural-language-processing algorithms", *[Scientific American](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")*, vol. 329, no. 4 (November 2023), pp. 81–82. "This murder mystery competition has revealed that although NLP ([natural-language processing](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles")) models are capable of incredible feats, their abilities are very much limited by the amount of [context](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") they receive. This [...] could cause [difficulties] for researchers who hope to use them to do things such as analyze [ancient languages](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles"). In some cases, there are few historical records on long-gone [civilizations](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") to serve as [training data](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") for such a purpose." (p. 82.)
- Daniel Jurafsky and James H. Martin (2008). *Speech and Language Processing*, 2nd edition. Pearson Prentice Hall. [ISBN](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [978-0-13-187321-6](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
- Mohamed Zakaria Kurdi (2016). *Natural Language Processing and Computational Linguistics: speech, morphology, and syntax*, Volume 1. ISTE-Wiley. [ISBN](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [978-1848218482](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
- Mohamed Zakaria Kurdi (2017). *Natural Language Processing and Computational Linguistics: semantics, discourse, and applications*, Volume 2. ISTE-Wiley. [ISBN](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles") [978-1848219212](/w/load.php?lang=en&modules=site.styles&only=styles&skin=vector-2022 "ResourceLoaderDynamicStyles").
- Christopher D. Manning, Prabhakar Raghavan, and Hinrich Schütze (2008). *Introduction to Information Retrieval*. Cambridge University Press.
